## Message 14979

- **ID:** 14979

- **Author:** Daily Bounty Writeups
- **Views:** 148

### Text

Still confused about how a real application works under the hood? 🤔

Most people know how to code, but have never actually visualized the complete end-to-end data flow.
In just 48 milliseconds, a single click travels through:
⚡ DNS & Edge CDN   
🛡️ Firewall & WAF security inspection   
⚖️ Load Balancer traffic routing   
🔐 API Gateway & JWT authentication   
🚀 Redis in-memory cache hits   
🗄️ PostgreSQL database commits   
📨 Kafka async event streams
Instead of looking at boring static diagrams, I built a 100% live interactive simulator where you can watch the packet animate across each layer in real time.

👉 Try the interactive visualizer here:

https://securitycipher.com/how-application-works/
